# 100s of AI Agents Running Live (Claude Code)

## Creator description

👉 Join AI Agent & Automation Builders (Creator Magic): https://mrc.fm/cmc
Lock your rate for life before the next price step.

Running 100s of AI agents live with Claude Code reviewing your tank PRs,
shipping new features and answering your questions in real time.

I'm live reviewing the flood of PRs you've been sending for tank - my
Claude Code command centre - merging your work, showing what's new, and
replying to your questions in real time. Hundreds of you activated tank
this week and I genuinely can't keep up (so yes, I'm hiring a senior dev -
details on stream).

Plus my take on two things that dropped this week: birdclaw (agent-friendly
X) and Sakana's Fugu orchestration model.

You get the actual tank repo inside the community and pull it home, run it on
your own infra, ship it for clients. Not a course. The source.
